================================================================
RENIXA COGNITIVE FOUNDATION, INC. 
CLUE TRANSCRIPT v1.2 OFFICIAL HEADER
----------------------------------------------------------------
Header text is unalienated part of the CLUE Transcript.
Any upstream alteration invalidates downstream processing.

Each module MUST preserve its XREF identifier
in format [DOCID|NNNNN].
================================================================
Syntax:
d:x| <SEGMENTS>

x ∈ {t, s}
t = transactional (event present)
s = stative (no state transition)

Segments are tagged atomic units:

- Agents segments
+A = active agent  ::= the agent that emits the action
+A = passive agent ::= the agent that receives/accepts/absorbs/etc. the results of the action

- Objects segments
O  = object/theme  ::= any objects involved into the action, besides the agents

- Verb segments
T  = transition/action
D  = stative predicate

- Cicumstantial segments
Cl = locative
Cs = subordinate clause
Cc = causal connector
Ct = temporal connector
Cp = purpose
Cy = qualifier
Ca = adjunct
Cm = manner
Cd = degree
================================================================
END OF THE OFFICIAL HEADER
